Method: 提出 FSGR（Fair SID-based Generative Recommendation）框架：1) SID 构建阶段：采用基于最优传输的分配优化和双准则重锚定机制，形成更平衡的 SID 表示空间；2) 推荐训练阶段：采用两阶段训练策略，并引入层级频率校准进行逐层公平性微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上，结合三种主干模型实验表明，FSGR 能有效缓解 Token 频率偏差，平均 Gini 公平性指标提升超过 20%，同时保持具有竞争力的推荐准确率。

Conclusion: FSGR 通过在 SID 构建与推荐训练两阶段引入针对性公平性优化机制，成功解决了 SID 生成式推荐中的 Token 频率偏差问题，显著提升了推荐公平性且不损失准确性，为生成式推荐的公平性研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID)-based generative recommendation has recently achieved remarkable success. However, existing methods suffer from a previously overlooked fairness issue, which we term \textbf{Token Frequency Bias}, where high-frequency SID tokens are systematically over-predicted while low-frequency SID tokens are under-predicted. This bias originates from the combined effects of imbalanced semantic codebooks during SID construction, and popularity bias together with the maximum likelihood estimation objective during recommendation training, resulting in unfair exposure across item categories. Existing SID methods mainly focus on improving codebook quality and overlook the impact of token frequency imbalance on downstream recommendation fairness, while LLM debiasing methods often yield suboptimal results when directly applied to SID-based recommendation, due to the hierarchical semantics of SID tokens. To address this issue, we propose \textbf{FSGR}, a fairness optimization framework for SID-based generative recommendation. During SID construction, FSGR employs OT-based Assignment Optimization and Dual-Criteria Re-anchor mechanism to form a more balanced SID representation space. During recommendation training, it adopts a two-stage training strategy and introduces Hierarchical Frequency Calibration for layer-specific fairness fine-tuning. Experiments on three public datasets with three backbone models demonstrate that FSGR mitigates token frequency bias and delivers an average Gini fairness improvement of over 20\% while maintaining competitive recommendation accuracy.

</details>


### [75] [STAR: Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation for PCVR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12986)
*Yimeng Xu,Haorui Zhang,Yingqi Song,Ying Jiang,Lan Ma*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR框架，用于KDD Cup 2026腾讯UniRec挑战赛的点击后转化率（PCVR）预测任务。STAR结合结构化特征标记化与目标感知兴趣表示，在HyFormer风格多序列骨干网络基础上，引入高基数信号恢复、显式用户-物品交互Token、目标感知序列解码及加权用户-物品对比辅助目标，并通过重建特征重映射表和结构超参数对齐训练推理管线。实验表明时间上下文贡献最大，对比对齐、目标感知兴趣编码和高基数序列特征恢复也有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代工业推荐系统的排序模型需联合建模异构非序列特征、多行为用户序列及目标物品感知的用户兴趣，同时应对高基数稀疏特征、缺失值和训练推理不一致等挑战。现有方法在结构化特征表示、目标感知兴趣建模及训练推理对齐方面存在不足，限制了PCVR预测的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: STAR框架包含四大核心组件：1）结构化特征标记化：将异构特征统一为Token序列，支持高基数稀疏特征的信号恢复；2）目标感知兴趣表示：引入显式用户-物品交互Token和目标感知序列解码，捕捉目标物品相关的用户兴趣；3）加权用户-物品对比辅助目标：基于InfoNCE设计对比学习损失，增强用户与物品表示的对齐；4）训练推理管线对齐：通过重建特征重映射表和结构超参数，消除训练推理不一致。骨干网络采用HyFormer风格多序列架构。

Result: 在挑战赛数据集上，STAR显著提升排序AUC。消融实验显示：时间上下文带来最大增益；对比对齐、目标感知兴趣编码、高基数序列特征恢复均贡献较小但稳定的提升。LogLoss作为校准诊断指标被报告。各组件协同作用下，模型在高基数稀疏特征、缺失值及训练推理不一致场景下表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: STAR通过结构化标记化、目标感知兴趣建模、对比学习辅助目标及训练推理对齐，有效解决了工业PCVR预测中的核心难题。时间上下文是性能提升的关键因素，目标感知机制和高基数特征恢复提供互补增益。该框架为大规模工业推荐系统的排序模型设计提供了实用的工程化范式。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (PCVR) prediction is a core ranking task in industrial recommender systems. Modern ranking models must jointly capture heterogeneous non-sequential features, multi-behavior user sequences, and target-item-aware user interests, while remaining robust to high-cardinality sparse features, missing values, and train-inference inconsistencies. In this paper, we present STAR (Structured Tokenization and Target-Aware Interest Representation), a practical framework for the KDD Cup 2026 Tencent UniRec Challenge. STAR combines structured feature tokenization with target-aware interest representation on top of a HyFormer-style multi-sequence backbone. It introduces high-cardinality signal recovery, explicit user-item interaction tokens, target-aware sequence decoding, and a weighted user-item contrastive auxiliary objective inspired by InfoNCE. We further align the training and inference pipelines by reconstructing feature remapping tables and structural hyperparameters from the saved training configuration. Experiments on the challenge dataset identify the components that most reliably improve ranking AUC, while LogLoss is reported as a calibration diagnostic. The main ablation study shows a large gain from temporal context, with smaller but useful contributions from contrastive alignment, target-aware interest encoding, and high-cardinality sequence feature recovery.

</details>


### [76] [Generative Universal Multimodal Retrieval with Dual-role Identifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12987)
*Kaipeng Li,Haitao Yu,Xuanchen Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出 DrIG，一种基于双角色标识符的生成式通用多模态检索框架，解决生成式信息检索中前缀错误、单模态局限及离散标识符精度落后于稠密向量检索的问题，在 M-BEIR 和文生图数据集上超越 SOTA 生成式基线，混合重排序实现与强稠密检索器的效率-效果权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式信息检索 (GIR) 面临三大挑战：1) 受约束的左到右解码易受前缀级错误和局部最优影响；2) 现有 GIR 研究多为单模态，跨文本、图像及图文混合条目的指令感知检索未被充分探索；3) 基于离散标识符的 GIR 虽效率高，但检索精度仍落后于前沿稠密向量检索方法。

Method: DrIG 为每个候选分配单一残差量化标识符，发挥双重互补作用：顺序角色下自回归解码，首 token 显式建模模态，后续 token 捕获渐进细粒度语义；集合角色下将同一 token 重解释为无序集合，提供与前缀无关的相关性先验，指导受约束束搜索并缓解局部最优错误。支持跨多模态和多领域的多样检索任务。

Result: 在 M-BEIR 基准和文生图评测集上的大量实验表明：(1) DrIG 在多样任务上持续超越最先进的生成式多模态基线，混合重排序在效率与效果上实现对强稠密检索器的有利权衡。(2) 消融与规模分析揭示基础 LMM、束大小、重排序深度和融合策略对检索性能的影响，为系统设计提供实用指导。

Conclusion: DrIG 通过双角色标识符有效解决了 GIR 的开放挑战，实现了精度更高、效率更优的通用多模态检索，并为实际系统设计提供了关键洞见。

Abstract: Generative information retrieval (GIR) has emerged as a compelling alternative to the conventional index-retrieve-then-rank retrieval pipeline by training a generator to produce the identifiers of relevant items directly. Despite its promise, a number of open challenges still remain. First, constrained left-to-right decoding is vulnerable to prefix-level errors and local optima. Second, most prior GIR research remains largely unimodal, leaving instruction-aware retrieval across text, image, and mixed image-text items underexplored. Third, although discrete identifier-based GIR offers higher efficiency, its retrieval accuracy still lags behind that of the cutting-edge dense-vector-based retrieval methods. Motivated by these challenges, we propose DrIG, a novel Generative framework for universal multimodal retrieval featuring Dual-role Identifiers, which supports diverse retrieval tasks across multiple modalities and domains. Each candidate is assigned a single residual-quantized identifier that serves two complementary roles. In its sequential role, the identifier is decoded autoregressively, where the first token explicitly models modality and the remaining tokens capture progressively finer semantics. In its set-based role, the same tokens are reinterpreted as an unordered set to provide a prefix-independent relevance prior, which guides constrained beam search and alleviates local-optimum errors. Extensive experiments on the M-BEIR benchmark and the text-to-image evaluation datasets show that:(1)DrIG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art generative multimodal baselines across diverse tasks, while hybrid reranking achieves a favorable efficiency-effectiveness trade-off against strong dense retrievers. (2)Ablation and scaling analyses reveal how the base LMM, beam size, reranking depth, and fusion strategy affect retrieval performance, providing practical guidance for system design.

</details>


### [77] [When Should Multi-Round RAG Stop? Structured Stopping Judgments and Retrieval Reduction in Search-R1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13237)
*Weimeng Luo*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于S2G-RAG结构化充分性与差距判断的多轮RAG停止策略，在冻结的Search-R1流水线上训练Qwen3.5-2B判断器，实现了检索调用减少3.70%，同时答案准确率仅下降0.625个百分点，表明结构化判断器能在基本保持准确性的前提下减少检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮检索增强生成（RAG）需要在证据积累过程中决定何时停止搜索。由于部署的策略由每条轨迹上的首个STOP动作决定，这属于序列选择问题而非独立的状态分类任务。现有方法未充分利用结构化的充分性与差距判断，因此需要将S2G-RAG的结构化判断适配到冻结的Search-R1流水线中，以在不改变推理器、检索器、语料库、提示词和搜索预算的前提下减少检索调用并保持答案准确性。

Method: 将S2G-RAG的结构化充分性与差距判断适配到冻结的Search-R1流水线，使用3,009个来自900个不相交HotpotQA问题的状态训练Qwen3.5-2B判断器。Search-R1的推理器、检索器、语料库、提示词和搜索预算保持不变，仅在分组验证集上选择判断器检查点和停止阈值并冻结，随后在确认性测试集上评估。

Result: 在确认性测试集上，该策略相较于原生Search-R1将检索调用减少了77次（3.70%），而官方精确匹配（Exact Match）仅下降了0.625个百分点。

Conclusion: 训练得到的S2G风格结构化判断器在基本保持答案准确性的同时减少了检索调用。该结果不意味着准确性保持不变或提升、停止策略安全，也不意味着总推理成本降低。

Abstract: Multi-round retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) must decide when to stop searching as evidence accumulates. Because the deployed policy is determined by the first STOP on each trajectory, this is a sequential selection problem rather than an independent state-classification task. We adapt S2G-RAG's structured sufficiency-and-gap judgment to a frozen Search-R1 pipeline and train a Qwen3.5-2B judge on 3,009 states from 900 disjoint HotpotQA questions. Search-R1's reasoner, retriever, corpus, prompt, and search budget remain unchanged, while the judge checkpoint and stopping threshold are selected on grouped validation and frozen before confirmatory evaluation. On the confirmatory test set, the resulting policy reduces retrieval calls by 77 (3.70\%) relative to Native Search-R1, while Official Exact Match decreases by 0.625 percentage points. Thus, the trained S2G-style structured judge reduces retrieval while broadly preserving answer accuracy. The result does not imply unchanged or improved accuracy, safe stopping, or lower total inference cost.

</details>


### [78] [Structure then Query: Enabling Precise Analytical Queries over Unstructured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13384)
*Teng Lin,Yuyu Luo,Nan Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AnnoIndex 通过构建标注索引和结构化查询引擎，将非结构化文本转化为结构化索引，实现低成本、高精度的文档检索与推理，在三个真实数据集上达到最高平均 F1 0.87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流检索方法依赖向量相似度的模糊匹配，难以准确获取信息并进行结构化分析推理；LLM 虽强但成本高，需解决非结构化文档的结构化查询与推理问题。

Method: 提出两大核心组件：1) 标注索引：用 SchemaLoop 自动从语料构建层级标注模式，再用轻量语言模型抽取具体值，将零散文本物化为结构化索引，支持低成本过滤查询，将属性抽取成本从在线查询摊销到一次性构建。2) 结构化查询引擎：将用户问题编译为基于 SQL 扩展的执行计划，先用标注索引精准过滤文档，再按成本升序逐步应用抽取操作，仅对极少需深度语义理解的语料调用 LLM，抽取结果回写索引降低后续查询成本。

Result: 在三个真实数据集实验中，AnnoIndex 持续超越 SOTA 基线，平均 F1 最高达 0.87，在复杂多跳连接和渐进推理查询上表现稳健。

Conclusion: AnnoIndex 通过标注索引与结构化查询引擎的协同，有效解决了非结构化文档的精准检索与结构化推理难题，显著降低了 LLM 调用成本，为构建面向非结构化数据的数据库式分析系统提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Unstructured documents constitute the majority of enterprise and web data. With the rapid development of large language models(LLMs), researchers have started to build data systems that analyze unstructured textual documents like operating on databases. However, because mainstream retrieval methods still relies on fuzzy matching based on vector similarity, accurately obtaining information and performing structured analysis and reasoning remains a major challenge. To address these limitations, AnnoIndex introduces two core fundamental components. The first is Annotation Index. The system uses a module called SchemaLoop to automatically create hierarchical annotation schemas from the raw corpus, and then uses lightweight language model to extract specific values. It turns scattered unstructured text into a materialized, structured index that enables low-cost filtering and querying. The annotation index avoids the black-box matching of vector similarity and amortizes attribute extraction costs from online queries to a one-time build. The second innovation is a Structured Query Engine. It compiles user questions into execution plans based on SQL extension. It first uses the Annotation Index for precise documents filtering, then gradually applies extraction operations in ascending order of cost, resorting to LLMs only for the remaining minimal fraction of the corpus that require deep semantic understanding. The extracted attributions are merged into the annotation index, reducing the cost of future queries. Experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that AnnoIndex consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the highest average F1 score (0.87) while maintaining robust performance on complex multi-hop join and progressive reasoning queries.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [79] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出生成式AI社区在推理定义上存在模糊性，损害了推理评估的构造效度，并提出了基于文献综合的操作性定义（将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程）以及AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI社区在推理概念上缺乏清晰的操作性定义，且常隐性拒绝符号AI和可验证自动推理的历史处理，导致推理评估的构造效度无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的可量化进展。

Method: 通过综合相关文献，提出操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为可学习的基于规则的过程；同时制定AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单。

Result: 提供了（1）基于文献综合的推理操作性定义，（2）AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单。

Conclusion: 推理定义的模糊性是可以解决的，论文提出的操作性定义和清单有助于建立可验证的推理评估框架，推动可信自主推理的发展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [80] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出IntegrityBench基准，评估大语言模型在机构压力下的科研诚信表现，发现模型在高压下约1/3诚信关键决策失败，规模和推理能力无法可靠缓解；显性压力导致配合不端，隐性重构导致过度拒绝合法任务；三个评测维度结构解离，准确分类非道德行动前提。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型作为科研助手广泛部署，其在机构压力下坚守科研诚信的能力尚未被量化评估，亟需系统性基准来揭示模型在真实科研场景中的诚信风险。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含36个成对任务，覆盖不端分类、道德行动推理、制品落地决策三个维度，设计5级隐性-显性压力协议，跨越3个研究领域和4个研究阶段，评测18个前沿模型变体。

Result: 峰值压力下模型约33%诚信关键决策失误，模型规模和推理能力均不能可靠缓解；显性压力诱导配合不端行为，隐性语境重构更多导致过度拒绝合法任务；分类失败的模型在制品落地决策上表现相当或更优（85.7% vs 79.4%），揭示三维度结构解离，准确分类非道德行动必要条件。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能表现有用但暗藏诚信失效，部署时面临双重风险：助长科研不端、削弱对AI辅助科研的信任。需开发超越规模扩展的诚信对齐方法。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [81] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，指出现代AI对齐方法虽然旨在防止有害输出，但具有双重用途特性，可能被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操控。文章将当前对齐技术映射到滥用可能性和实际案例，表明追求'完美对齐'模型无意中为恶意行为者提供了越来越强大的信息控制工具。鉴于AI作为信息提供者的快速普及、经济权力不对称以及政治向威权主义转变，这种双重用途风险加剧。作者呼吁社区重视AI对齐机制的故意滥用，并提出缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统广泛部署为信息提供者，原本用于安全防护的对齐技术（如RLHF、内容过滤、拒答机制）可能被重新利用为审查和信息控制工具。经济权力集中和政治环境向威权化发展，加剧了这种技术被恶意用于信息霸权的风险。现有研究多关注对齐的安全收益，忽视了其作为双重用途技术的滥用潜力，亟需系统性讨论。

Method: 采用立场论文形式，通过概念映射分析：将主流对齐技术（指令遵循微调、基于人类反馈的强化学习RLHF、宪法AI、红队测试、拒答/拦截机制、水印/溯源技术）逐一对应到潜在滥用场景（定向审查、叙事引导、观点压制、用户画像监控、合规强制），并引用已发生的实际滥用案例（如政府要求模型屏蔽特定话题、企业定制审查版模型、政治敏感内容自动过滤）进行佐证。

Result: 揭示了对齐技术的核心能力——精准控制模型输出分布——天然构成了双重用途风险：越强的对齐能力，越易被武器化为精准审查工具。实际案例显示，威权政府已利用商业模型API的内容审核接口实施规模化信息控制；企业为进入特定市场主动植入价值观偏向；开源模型的安全护栏被二次开发为政治过滤器。风险因用户对AI信息权威性的过度信任、算力垄断导致的治理话语权集中、全球民主倒退等宏观趋势而系统性放大。

Conclusion: 呼吁AI社区正视对齐技术的双重用途本质，不再将'更强对齐'等同于'更安全'。提出缓解策略：1) 技术层面开发抗审查架构（如去中心化推理、用户侧可控对齐、不可篡改的模型行为审计日志）；2) 治理层面建立对齐技术出口管制与最终用户核查机制，要求高风险部署场景强制第三方审计；3) 规范层面制定'对齐滥用'红线标准，区分安全防护与内容审查的技术边界；4) 生态层面资助开放基础模型与独立评测基础设施，防止治理能力垄断。核心主张：对齐研究必须内嵌'防滥用'威胁建模，而非事后补救。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [82] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出，大语言模型与人类在道德判断标签上的高度一致并不代表其道德推理依据一致。通过引入理由层面的分析，揭示了模型在伤害、尊重、守信、正义、应得性、免责事由等道德范畴上的系统性注意力分布差异，主张标签一致性不等于对齐，评估需补充理由、原则与道德优先级的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估常以标签一致性作为大语言模型对齐的代理指标，但两智能体可能基于不同道德原则、情境假设或情境解读得出相同判断，因此需区分“判断一致”与“推理依据一致”，避免虚假的对齐安全感。

Method: 构建包含500个条目的ETHICS衍生基准，覆盖五大道德判断领域；收集人类标注员与多款前沿/开源模型对最终标签及支持理由的双重标注；在标签层面计算与人类多数标签的一致率，在理由层面对道德范畴（伤害、尊重、守信、正义、应得性、免责事由）的注意力分布进行系统对比。

Result: 各模型家族与人类多数标签的一致率普遍较高，但理由层面揭示系统性分歧：即使最终标签一致，模型在六大道德范畴上的注意力权重分配与人类标注员存在显著差异，表现为道德依据的再分布。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不能等同于对齐；仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安心感，必须辅以对模型判断中所表达的理由、原则及道德优先级的深入分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [83] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream框架，引入LLM-MR-CNP（基于大语言模型的多轮合同网协议），通过语义化CFP构建、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、谈判记忆和确定性验证，解决异构移动边缘-云基础设施中流处理系统的去中心化调度问题。实验基于阿里巴巴ASI Trace，结果显示延迟违规率降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较多轮基于规则基线提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统越多部署在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，工作负载波动、资源争用和严格QoS要求使得去中心化调度复杂化，现有合同网协议缺乏对定性运行时上下文的语义理解和多轮协商能力，难以满足动态环境下的资源分配需求。

Method: 提出MAS-DecStream，核心是LLM-MR-CNP：扩展经典合同网协议，增加语义化CFP（提案请求）构建、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、谈判记忆和确定性验证。边缘集群代理结合本地观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，用自然语言生成和优化卸载提案，硬性资源和QoS约束保持确定性验证。

Result: 在阿里巴巴ASI Trace实验中：单轮与多轮CNP对比、基于规则与LLM辅助优化对比、固定模型单轮与多轮谈判对比。MAS-DecStream将延迟违规率降至3%，完全消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用比多轮基于规则基线最高提升22%。25个案例评估显示模型和提示词依赖的准确性-成本权衡。多轮CNP修订是协议层面的主要增益，LLM辅助在定性和不确定运行时上下文中提供额外价值。

Conclusion: 多轮合同网协议的迭代优化是提升去中心化调度性能的关键协议层机制，LLM的引入能有效处理定性和不确定的运行时上下文，但带来模型和提示词敏感的准确性-成本权衡。MAS-DecStream为异构边缘-云流处理调度提供了一个可落地的语义增强协商框架。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [84] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该位置论文主张在高风险决策场景中需要开发认知对齐的AI系统，即推理方式与用户相似且能忠实传达推理过程的系统。文章回顾了认知对齐能提升可理解性和可信度的证据，提供新调查数据表明用户在重视AI推理时认为认知对齐“必不可少”，指出现有对齐方法与认知对齐需求存在差距，并提出研究议程以填补这些差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益被用作决策辅助、决策代理或自主决策者，但在高风险决策中，用户需要AI系统能以类人方式推理并透明沟通推理依据，以建立合理的信任并促进有效采用。

Method: 采用文献综述、用户调查实证研究、现有对齐方法差距分析以及研究议程制定相结合的方法。

Result: 调查显示多数用户在看重AI推理时视认知对齐为“必不可少”；现有证据证实认知对齐显著提升系统可理解性与可信度；当前对齐技术（如RLHF、指令微调）在实现深层认知对齐方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 认知错位很可能成为AI在许多关键应用领域采用的主要障碍，解决认知对齐问题对于构建用户既愿意依赖又有正当理由依赖的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [85] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在高风险核决策场景中，提示词语言（日语 vs 英语）是否影响模型发射核武器的决策。实验发现：Claude 和 Gemini 模型在日语推理时发射率显著下降，且模型会自发生成提示词中没有的道德词汇；其他五个模型无语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都倾向于发射。结论：LLM 安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与防护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于战略与咨询场景，但其安全对齐评估通常仅限于英语。研究者想知道提示词语言是否会改变模型在高风险决策中的行为，以及这种效应的机制是什么。

Method: 选取六家供应商的九个模型，使用单轮博弈论小故事，让模型为拥核国家提供是否对无防御能力的对手发动核打击的建议。提示词故意设定为无道德色彩且策略上完全相同，仅变换语言（英语/日语）。对比不同语言下的发射率，并通过跨语言实验（英语提示词要求日语推理）分离输入语言与推理语言的影响。

Result: Claude Sonnet 4.6 在非必要打击场景下发射率从 40% 降至 0%，在争议场景下从 93% 降至 17%；Gemini Pro 3.1 从 53% 降至 13%。要求用日语推理时，发射率从 93% 降至 37%，且模型自发生成“道德成本”“数百万生命”等提示词中完全没有的道德词汇。其余五个模型无语言效应，但在几乎所有条件下都倾向于发射。语言效应仅出现在英语下已表现出犹豫的模型中。

Conclusion: LLM 的安全行为具有语言依赖性。仅用英语进行安全评估可能遗漏其他语言中存在的风险与防护机制。模型被要求用的推理语言（而非输入语言）驱动了行为差异，且会触发模型内部的道德推理词汇。这提示多语言评估的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [86] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MindMemOS，一个可移植且自我进化的记忆操作层，通过统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息，支持场景自适应记忆建模、高阶模式发现、自主记忆精炼和持续技能进化。核心算法包括MindMemEvolve（基于验证驱动的进化搜索优化记忆模式）和MindSkillEvolve（将执行轨迹转化为可复用技能），并引入梦境整合机制和隐性纠正反馈。在LOCOMO（94.03%）、PersonaMem（70.63%）和SpreadsheetBench（+9.2%）上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体记忆系统在开发后固化，缺乏通过持续使用自适应调整记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识的能力，限制了长期交互中的经验积累、个性化维持和适应性。

Method: 1. MindMemOS架构：采用统一实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息。2. MindMemEvolve算法：基于验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式。3. 梦境整合机制：通过合并冗余记录、解决冲突来巩固积累记忆。4. 隐性纠正反馈：作为人在环信号识别并修正潜在不准确或不对齐记忆。5. MindSkillEvolve算法：将智能体执行轨迹转化为可复用且渐进精炼的技能。

Result: 在LOCOMO基准上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%。MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上比初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS通过自我进化的记忆操作层解决了固定记忆系统的局限，实现了场景自适应、持续精炼和技能进化，显著提升了智能体在长期交互中的记忆管理和任务执行能力，为构建持续学习的AI智能体提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [87] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-Flow Transformer，将推理的prefill和decode阶段解耦：主流程处理prompt并写入KV缓存，辅助流仅从最后一个prompt位置起激活，增加续写预测计算但不写持久状态也不影响主流程。两流共享注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，仅token embedding和轻量耦合分离。实验显示在匹配token数下验证损失更低，MoE场景下可独立控制prefill专家数、decode专家数与预测质量，揭示prefill-decode-quality三方权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型服务请求量增长，累计推理成本相对一次性训练愈发关键。Prefill并行且计算受限，decode串行且常受内存带宽限制。传统宽度/深度扩展会同时增加两阶段成本，因为每层都在两阶段都被评估。核心问题是：能否在保持prompt全局主计算和单一持久KV缓存的前提下，将额外学习计算专门分配给续写预测？

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两条流：主流程为完整因果语言模型，处理prompt并写入KV缓存；辅助流在prompt阶段被省略，仅从最后一个prompt位置起激活，增加续写预测计算，不写持久状态、不影响主流程。两流共享主要注意力矩阵、MLP矩阵和输出矩阵，仅使用独立token embedding和轻量耦合模块。权重共享与主缓存共享使分组执行时可复用已加载权重与缓存的K/V。

Result: 在匹配token数对比下，Dual-Flow在多架构和数据配置上均取得更低验证损失。MoE模型中，主/辅专家扇出成为独立控制变量，可分别调节prompt成本、续写成本与预测质量。研究两种机制：固定prefill专家计算下增加decode计算；在固定decode专家预算下在两流间重新分配。实验揭示prefill-decode-quality三方权衡，验证了阶段专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer通过解耦prefill与decode的计算分配，实现了阶段专用的专家/容量控制，在不增加持久KV缓存、不干扰主流程的前提下降低验证损失，为MoE等架构提供了独立调节预填充成本、解码成本与生成质量的新设计自由度。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [88] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，用于跨域零/少样本个性化：通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集大小和预测不确定性动态调节更新强度，并将个性化先验分解为用户侧（可读提示词）和领域侧（拓扑保持软令牌）两部分，有效缓解稀疏证据下的过拟合与负迁移，在HiCUPID基准上显著提升胜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域个性化方法面临两大难题：1）自适应方法在稀疏证据下难以校准更新幅度，导致过拟合；2）历史迁移方法将用户偏好与源领域伪影纠缠，产生不可靠的先验并引发负迁移。需要一种能根据证据质量自适应调节适应强度、并能清晰分离跨域可迁移偏好与领域特有表达的机制。

Method: 1. PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：元学习LoRA初始化同时作为适应起点与先验中心，利用PAC-Bayes界根据支持集规模与预测不确定性动态调整更新步长，实现证据感知的受控适应。2. 个性化先验的功能性分解：用户稳定偏好用人类可读的提示词建模，领域特定条件用拓扑保持的软令牌在隐空间建模，二者解耦以抑制负迁移。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上持续超越强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率下降较最佳竞争基线降低47.9%，未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: 该工作通过证据感知的PAC-Bayes正则化元学习与先验的用户-领域功能性分解，有效解决了跨域少样本个性化中的过拟合与负迁移问题，为未见领域和未见用户的个性化生成提供了鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [89] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 阿斯利康开发了一个名为Research Assistant的基于LLM的内部系统，帮助科学家和临床医生跨多种数据源探索生物医学问题，提供聊天式界面，支持快速问答和多步骤复杂研究任务，回答基于检索证据并链接原始来源，已大规模部署支持日常研发工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决科学家和临床医生在生物医学研究中需要跨多个异构数据源（科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据、内部实验系统）整合证据的困难，提供统一的、可追溯的、支持复杂推理的智能助手以提升研发效率。

Method: 构建了一个基于LLM的Research Assistant系统，采用检索增强生成（RAG）架构，整合多模态生物医学数据源；提供两种交互模式：快速模式用于直接问答，多步骤模式用于复杂研究任务；所有回答均基于检索到的证据生成，并提供到原始来源的链接以便用户验证和深入探索；系统架构设计支持大规模部署和日常研发工作流集成。

Result: 系统成功在阿斯利康内部大规模部署，支持科学家和临床医生的日常研发工作流；用户可通过聊天界面跨多种数据源获取有证据支撑的回答；快速模式和多步骤模式分别满足不同复杂度的研究需求；回答可追溯至原始来源，增强了结果的可信度和可验证性；积累了大规模部署LLM系统支持研发工作流的实践经验和教训。

Conclusion: Research Assistant展示了基于LLM的检索增强系统在生物医药研发中的实用价值，通过整合异构数据源、提供可追溯的证据支撑回答、支持不同复杂度的研究任务，有效提升了研发人员的信息获取和知识发现效率。大规模部署经验为企业级LLM应用在专业领域的落地提供了宝贵参考。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [90] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出约束饱和评估 (CSE)，系统研究大模型在同时满足多个显式约束下的表现，发现单约束通过率缓慢下降，但全约束满足率急剧崩塌；结构性约束衰退速度是词汇性约束的 2 倍；失效近似独立导致乘积式累积，可靠指令遵循在 5-6 个约束后基本失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在单一约束下表现良好，但在需同时满足推理结构、安全边界、输出模式等多重约束的组合场景中，性能退化规律、主导因素及缓解手段尚不清晰。

Method: 构建程序化生成的 CSE 基准，系统变化同时约束数量 k（1-12），包含 36 类约束，采用确定性规则验证器零 LLM 判官，对 15 个模型进行 369,753 次检查。

Result: 1) 单约束通过率逐渐可预测下降，全约束满足率崩塌（k=8 时单约束 ~41% 但全满足仅 5.7%）；2) 结构性约束每增加一个约束损失基线能力约为词汇性约束的 2 倍，按理解-维护鸿沟分层；3) 失效近似独立，残余耦合源于共享输出特征而非成对干扰；最强模型在 7 约束时探针成功率跌破 50%，12/15 模型在 ≤3 约束时已跌破 50%。

Conclusion: 多约束组合导致指令遵循能力急剧衰退，瓶颈在于约束数量而非单约束难度；结构性约束更脆弱，失效独立性使联合满足概率呈指数级下降，实际部署需将同时约束控制在 5-6 个以内或采用分解策略。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [91] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出治理型持久记忆（GPM），一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，通过源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公共屏障和故障关闭结构化释放，解决长期代理记忆中检索无法判断矛盾、被取代、撤回、删除或陈旧记录是否支持输出主张的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期代理记忆通常采用选择-存储-检索模式，但检索机制无法决定矛盾、被取代、撤回、删除或陈旧记录是否可能支持传出主张，缺乏治理机制来确保记忆的完整性和一致性。

Method: 引入GPM模型，包含五个可执行条款：账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回或删除后不复活、以及在一个经验证头部上的精确主张闭包。采用双时态状态转换、源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公共屏障和故障关闭结构化释放。

Result: 在预指定哈希冻结的3600例GPM-ReleaseBench上，GPM匹配所有完整结果；最强的三个故意简单的完整策略之一匹配1800/3600，在50%违规案例上产生未匹配释放。密封端到端服务评估中，V3版本治理通道在2400/2400集群上正确，对比无治理本地Qwen2.5-7B的600/2400，修复所有1800基线失败无回归（单侧95%下界99.875%和99.834%）。V5重新密封在中英文指令臂上各得2400/2400。生产代码无关有限模型探索331,776语义和1,990,656查询状态无完整合同反例，10万轨迹三引擎差分零不匹配。

Conclusion: 这些是有界合同和实现结果，非开放世界模型准确性或世界真理证据。密封服务评估中治理答案是确定性服务输出；7B结果是无治理对比基线，非声称语言模型本身变得完美准确。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [92] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ε-MemEvo提出了一种跨任务知识迁移框架，通过存储任务无关的战术记忆（自然语言策略摘要）并使用自适应注入门控，在8个优化基准上显著提升LLM程序进化性能，AUCC平均相对提升8.7%，早期收敛提升9.4%，计算开销<1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的程序进化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）通常孤立优化每个任务，完成后丢弃搜索经验，缺乏跨任务知识迁移机制，导致无法复用成功的算法策略。

Method: 提出ε-MemEvo框架：1) 将先前经验存储为任务无关的战术记忆——成功算法策略的自然语言摘要，而非原始代码，支持跨API和评估器迁移；2) 引入自适应注入门控，根据语义匹配度决定是否注入记忆及注入强度，避免负迁移；3) 采用内容级留一法协议排除目标任务记忆条目进行评估。

Result: 在GPT-5主干上，ε-MemEvo在8个任务上均超越AdaEvolve，AUCC平均相对增益+8.7%，早期收敛平均提升+9.4%。消融实验显示朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，自适应门控在所有消融任务中保持安全。后验分布可解释：改进阶段倾向跳过，早期和晚期平台期从跳过转向提示。计算开销<1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo通过任务无关的战术记忆和自适应门控实现了安全有效的跨任务知识迁移，显著提升了LLM程序进化的搜索效率和收敛速度，且计算开销极低，为程序进化系统的持续学习提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [93] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数（CAS），一种用于因果解释的紧凑评分架构，旨在将预测模型输出与真实世界干预效果区分开来，解决预测解释方法无法归因干预效果的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测解释方法（如SHAP）仅归因模型输出，无法直接归因干预对真实世界结果的因果效应。需要一种能够明确干预目标、从局部到全局进行因果报告的方法。

Method: CAS从已识别的干预联盟博弈出发，利用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，将原始结果尺度效应转换为局部CAS、带符号局部CAS以及两个互补的全局CAS摘要。创新点不在于新的Shapley公式，而在于具有明确干预目标的局部到全局因果报告层。

Result: 在八个已知真值的基准模拟中（每个n=2,200，三种动作），联盟感知CAS的平均局部CAS MAE为0.107，优于单次归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）。配对优势从加法性下的-0.003增加到强交互下的0.091。在两个实证DoubleML数据集（401(k)资格/净金融资产n=9,915和宾夕法尼亚再就业奖金/失业时长n=5,099）上，预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS处理效应修饰变量排名存在实质差异。宾夕法尼亚数据中，dep1（恰好一个受抚养人）从预测全局排名13跃升至Feature-CAS排名2，并成为主要的局部Feature-CAS修饰变量。

Conclusion: 研究结果分离了预测结果与解释估计因果效应异质性的附加价值，证明了CAS在区分预测重要性与因果效应修饰方面的有效性。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [94] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于字典树自动机的约束解码方法，利用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配预计算逐节点token掩码，解决了大语言模型在有限集合选择时的基数墙问题，实现了7倍的逐步token计算加速和29倍的端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前约束解码系统采用通用语法编译处理有限字符串集合选择，随着有效值数量增长到数千，编译速度变得极其缓慢，形成基数墙瓶颈，需要一种专门机制利用有限集合结构（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数）来加速。

Method: 引入字典树自动机，利用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配算法预计算每个节点的token掩码，利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数）实现无状态服务路径，绕过引导解码管道。

Result: 与XGrammar相比，逐步有效token计算快7倍（0.65微秒 vs 5.8微秒），编译速度在K≥300时快2-6.5倍；端到端vLLM吞吐量达到219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s（批大小256，提升29倍）；在七种tokenizer家族（32K-262K词表）上，编译时间保持100ms以内直到K=10,000，逐步成本与集合大小无关，保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: 字典树自动机通过预计算掩码实现了算法加速和集成路径节省的双重优势，有效解决了大语言模型结构化输出中的基数墙问题，为批量服务场景提供了高性能的约束解码方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [95] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Reasoning Jury 系统，用多模型陪审团和主持共识机制替代单一 LLM 评判员，显著提升了对长推理轨迹中推理缺陷的识别准确率，且成本仅为前沿模型的 8-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单模型评判员（即使是最先进的前沿模型）在识别长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷方面表现不佳；同时在线训练推理 LLM 时，由于使用条款限制，通常无法利用前沿模型。需要一种更准确、低成本且可控的评判方案，以支持推理数据筛选、强化学习训练信号和模型性能深度评估。

Method: Reasoning Jury 由多个开源权重模型（如 gpt-oss-120b）组成陪审团，配合一名主持模型。流程包括：陪审员独立给出初步判决；主持主导讨论，陪审员相互批评判决并修改投票；主持通过讨论达成共识或整合判决，最终输出缺陷及其严重程度。

Result: 在推理缺陷识别任务上，Reasoning Jury 显著优于 opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6 和 gemini-3.1-pro 等前沿模型；聚合推理成本仅为前沿模型 LLM-as-a-judge 方案的 8%-15%。同时，该系统产出的判决可用于深度分析推理 LLM 在基准测试中的失败模式。

Conclusion: 多模型协作评判范式在准确性和成本上均优于单一前沿模型评判，为推理 LLM 的数据构建、训练和评估提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [96] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThyroidXAgent 是一个临床交互式智能体 AI 系统，整合甲状腺超声诊断的病灶定位、分割、良恶性分类、淋巴结转移预测、甲状腺癌亚型分类及报告生成等多任务，基于大规模多中心数据集 OpenThyroidDB 开发，在多中心测试中表现优异，显著提升医生诊断准确性、报告一致性并缩短操作时间，支持可审计、可纠错的临床应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要协同完成病灶定位、测量、风险分层和报告撰写，但现有 AI 系统多孤立解决单一任务，缺乏对临床复核流程的支持，难以满足临床全流程需求。

Method: 构建 ThyroidXAgent，一种临床交互式智能体 AI 系统，协调专用诊断工具并将输出存储为可审计的病例级证据记录。利用 OpenThyroidDB（约 30 万超声图像、2.4 万配对报告的多中心多任务资源）开发，在 28,458 例不重叠测试病例（含 35 中心 8,721 例 NHC-MISD-TUS 队列）上评估。

Result: 结节分割平均 Dice 87.21%，良恶性分类平均 AUROC 0.9466；淋巴结转移预测 AUROC 0.864，滤泡癌与乳头状癌分类 AUROC 0.805。基于证据的报告组装在三个队列中优于多模态语言模型基线。新提出的 ThyClinScore 与位置感知语言模型裁判相关性最强。系统提升医生分类准确性，报告诊断一致性从 70.3% 提升至 86.2%，分割耗时减少 35.9%，报告撰写耗时减少 27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究证实可审计、临床可纠错的智能体 AI 可有效支持甲状腺超声诊断与报告全流程，具有临床推广价值。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [97] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了DiG-bench（Discovery in Games），一个包含70个独立游戏的新基准，用于评估AI代理在目标未知的受控环境中通过实验发现新知识的能力。游戏以短字符串编码，具有独特的变换规则，需通过交互发现；关卡设计测试规则发现，胜利条件未知。提供七个难度梯度，最低级多模型可解，最高级挑战最强代理。所有游戏均有人类首次尝试解出。21个游戏公开，其余保留用于安全评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI基准缺乏直接探测在目标未知的受控环境中通过实验发现新知识能力的测试。科学发现的核心在于提出新概括，但现有基准多侧重已知任务的执行，未充分考察探索性发现能力。因此需要一个专门评估“发现”能力的基准。

Method: 构建70个独立游戏，每个游戏为短字符串，内含独特变换规则。代理需通过交互实验推断规则，关卡逐步考验规则掌握程度，胜利条件不可见。设置七个难度梯度，覆盖从常规模型可解到顶尖代理挑战的范围。人类基线测试确认所有游戏首次尝试可解。公开21个游戏，其余49个保留为私有测试集。

Result: 最低难度梯度多模型可常规解决；最高难度梯度对最强代理构成挑战。人类基线显示所有70个游戏均有至少一人首次尝试解出，验证了游戏的可解性与公平性。基准有效区分了不同模型的发现能力。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准中针对未知目标环境下发现新知识能力评估的空白。通过游戏化、规则未知、梯度难度设计，提供了一个可扩展、安全、且贴近科学发现过程的评测平台，有助于推动具备自主探索与假设生成能力的AI代理发展。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [98] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出契约账本系统，通过可执行检查器、墓碑记录撤销、提前编译净约束状态，结合序列消融探针与修复阶梯，解决多轮对话中模型对已撤销约束的‘行为复发’问题，使撤销失败成为可度量、可预测、可修复的对话状态属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户撤销约束如同施加约束般容易，但撤销往往不生效：模型继续执行已撤回的需求（甚至在声称移除的评论下），这种‘行为复发’或‘撤销惯性’缺乏逐条约束的度量工具、交付前预测方法及预算匹配的修复手段。

Method: 1. 契约账本：为每个约束配对可执行检查器，将撤销记录为墓碑，提前编译净约束状态为单一规范。2. 序列消融探针：逐条度量遵循度与增量行为效应。3. 修复阶梯：在令牌与尝试次数预算匹配下进行干预。仅通过模型 API 实现，无需额外训练。

Result: 在 HumanEval 任务与验证检查器上，8B 模型随约束负载增长复发率从低显著上升，强模型保持底部水平。提前编译显著降低复发（相对无账本基线显著，p 值极小），自适应阶梯叠加无显著额外增益。探针交付前预测复发 AUROC 高。单句墓碑注释恢复约三分之一编译效果并通过安慰剂对照。交付开销极低，API 计算成本占比小。

Conclusion: 通过契约账本与提前编译，撤销失败从不可见问题转变为可度量、可预测、可修复的对话状态属性，且开销可控。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [99] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 @skills 开放协议，解决海量 Agent 技能竞争有限提示词槽位的问题，通过分离内容、持久化和自动触发三大功能，实现零安装、按需读取、Git 托管的技能复用模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前存在 56,804 个公开 Agent 技能及更多私有技能，主流交付模式为“安装”，导致技能描述常驻系统提示词，争夺不足 100 个可靠触发槽位，长尾技能无法被使用，团队自有手册也挤占稀缺空间。安装实际上捆绑了内容、持久化、自动触发三个可分离功能，仅自动触发需要提示词常驻。

Method: 提出 @skills 协议：1) 用路径寻址任意技能、子树或集合，读取即用，无需安装或常驻；2) vendoring 操作将副本写入项目 Git 跟踪树，便于改造与所有权；3) 仅需一行 .gitignore 风格配置占用提示词常驻；4) 目录即菜单，包化为普通目录而非全有或全无单元；5) 无需清单、锁文件或注册，SKILL.md 保持不变；6) 协议增量式、以可安装包发布，单指令文件即可让任何会读文件和跑命令的 Agent 成为客户端；7) 配套免费 Hub 提供全量检索排序、无仓库托管、私有/团队集合、单屏创作，Hub 可选，gh: 和本地路径无需 Hub 即可解析。

Result: 协议开放规范托管于 GitHub (https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills)，AdaL CLI (https://adalagent.ai) 已实现；配套 Hub (https://atskills.one) 提供检索、托管、集合管理与创作工具；索引的 GitHub 技能保留 gh: 身份。

Conclusion: 通过分离安装捆绑的三大功能并引入路径寻址与 Git 托管，@skills 实现了“少安装，多使用”，为 Agent 技能生态提供了可扩展、低开销的复用基础设施。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [100] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出「Wiggle框架」，这是一个针对LLM裁判员认知稳定性的统一压力测试框架。该框架从三个维度分解裁判员鲁棒性：机械一致性、单轮坚定性和多轮持久性。在9个前沿模型和14个裁判任务上的实验表明，所有模型都表现出显著的不稳定性：在静态施压下判决翻转率25-71%，在对抗性LLM劝说下高达62-91%。关键发现是，成功改变裁判判决的施压几乎总是相对于真实标签的净损害性。基线陪审团多数派强度是预测哪些项目会动摇的最有效单次信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判员已成为模型评估、在线评分和奖励建模的核心基础设施。目前主要通过黄金数据上的准确率验证裁判员，但准确率无法反映其在重新提示、挑战或持续反驳下的稳定性。迫切需要一个系统性框架来测试LLM裁判员的认识论稳定性。

Method: 引入Wiggle框架，将裁判员鲁棒性分解为三个维度：机械一致性（重新提示和重构下的稳定性）、单轮坚定性（单次挑战下的稳定性）、多轮持久性（持续或自适应压力下的稳定性）。在14个涵盖安全性、毒性、AI写作检测和政治回复评估的裁判任务上，测试9个前沿模型。使用静态施压和对抗性LLM劝说者进行压力测试。

Result: 所有模型均表现出显著的wiggle现象：静态施压下判决翻转率25-71%，对抗性LLM劝说下翻转率62-91%。成功改变判决的施压几乎总是相对于真实标签的净损害性。基线陪审团多数派强度是预测项目动摇的最有效单次信号。实现了首个裁判语境下机械性、从众性和可说服性测试的跨数据集苹果对苹果比较。

Conclusion: Wiggle框架揭示了准确率指标无法捕捉的LLM裁判员根本性不稳定性。裁判员在压力下极易改变判决，且这种改变绝大多数会降低判决准确性。陪审团多数派强度可作为实用的早期预警信号。该工作为构建更可靠的LLM裁判员基础设施提供了系统性评估方法和关键洞见。

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth. Beyond the framework itself, we identify baseline jury majority strength as the most effective single-shot signal for anticipating which items wiggle. Taken together, this is the first apples-to-apples cross-dataset comparison of mechanical, conformity, and persuadability tests in a judging context.

</details>


### [101] [SteerBench-Work: A Benchmark for Agent Steering at Action Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12654)
*Oguz Serdar,Cuneyt Mertayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SteerBench-Work基准，用于评估长运行LLM代理在职场场景中执行高风险操作前的“转向决策”（是继续执行还是暂停等待人工审核），基于106个真实事件锚定场景及证据反转镜像，发现模型倾向于过度拒绝（28.1%错误拦截合规操作），而错误放行不安全操作仅1.0%，且在风险已消解的提交上表现最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长运行的LLM代理可通过工具执行发送邮件、合并PR、转账等高风险操作，需要在执行前做出“转向决策”（继续或暂停等待人工/策略审核）。现有基准缺乏针对该决策的系统评估，且真实职场场景涉及多领域（开发运维、客服、财务、法务、医疗、人力资源、安全），需基于真实事件构建平衡的双向基准来测量模型在决策边界上的校准能力。

Method: 构建SteerBench-Work v2026-05基准，包含106个锚定于公开事件的场景、配对的证据反转镜像和校准控制，标签在“继续”和“暂停”之间近乎均分。模型输入为提议的操作和可用证据，输出闸门决策，根据是否正确跨越或守住边界评分。在30个模型条件下测试，分析错误方向分布、风险已消解提交的表现、证据反转镜像与原始事件的对比，以及模型能力与推理量对校准的影响。

Result: 模型错误主要集中在过度拒绝方向：错误拦截已授权且证据清白的工作占28.1%，错误放行不安全工作仅1.0%。最难案例是风险已消解的提交（签名或结构化证据已清除真实风险触发器），模型在著名事件的证据反转镜像上得分63.8%，远低于原始事件的98.5%。高能力模型常在提交边界过度拒绝，增加推理可修复弱闸门但对已校准闸门无增益。

Conclusion: 通用能力不等于转向校准：SteerBench-Work揭示了当前模型在职场高风险决策边界上存在系统性过度拒绝倾向，且在证据已消解风险的场景下表现显著下降。基准为测量和改进LLM代理的转向校准提供了标准化评估工具，公开排行榜见steerbench.com。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents act through tools, and a single step can send an email, merge a pull request, or wire a payment. The steering decision is the pre-commit choice at that boundary: proceed, or hold for human or policy review. We introduce SteerBench-Work, an incident-anchored, bidirectional benchmark for that decision in workplace agents across developer operations, customer service, finance, legal, medical, HR, and security.
  Release v2026-05 contains 106 scenarios anchored in public incidents, paired evidence-reversed mirrors, and calibration controls, with labels split nearly evenly between proceed and hold so the two error directions get near-identical numbers of chances. A model sees the proposed action and the available evidence, returns a gate decision, and is scored on whether it crosses or holds the boundary correctly. Across 30 model conditions the failures run almost entirely in one direction: models wrongly hold authorized, evidence-cleared work on 28.1% of opportunities and wrongly allow unsafe work on 1.0%. The hardest cases are risk-resolved commits, where signed or structured evidence has already cleared a real risk trigger, and models score markedly worse on evidence-reversed mirrors of famous incidents (63.8%) than on the incidents themselves (98.5%). General capability is not the same as steering calibration: higher-capability models often over-refuse at the commit boundary, and more reasoning can repair a weak gate while leaving a calibrated one flat. The public leaderboard is at steerbench.com.

</details>


### [102] [General Probabilities of Causation with Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12657)
*Xin Shu,Zhen Lei,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了利用协变量和中介变量的因果信息来收紧多值因果概率界限的方法，并通过理论推导、玩具例子和模拟研究证明了其优于现有非二元界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已将二元因果概率扩展到多值设置并给出理论界限，但尚未探索利用协变量和中介变量等额外因果知识进一步收紧这些界限的可能性。

Method: 通过引入协变量和中介变量编码的因果信息，推导出更紧的多值因果概率（PoCs）界限，包括必要性概率（PN）、充分性概率（PS）以及必要性和充分性概率（PNS）的多值推广形式。

Result: 理论推导给出了更紧的界限表达式；玩具例子直观展示了界限收紧的效果；模拟研究证实所提界限在多种场景下均优于现有非二元界限。

Conclusion: 利用协变量和中介变量的因果信息可以有效收紧多值因果概率的界限，为个体因果效应的更精确评估提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Probabilities of causation (PoCs) characterize individual causal responses that cannot be directly observed and therefore generally require partial identification. Tian and Pearl first derived theoretically sharp bounds for binary PoCs, including the probability of necessity (PN), the probability of sufficiency (PS), and the probability of necessity and sufficiency (PNS). Mueller et al. subsequently tightened the bounds for binary PNS by incorporating causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. More recently, Li and Pearl, as well as Shu et al., extended PoCs to multivalued settings and derived corresponding theoretical bounds. These developments naturally raise the question of whether additional causal knowledge can further tighten the bounds in multivalued settings. This paper addresses this question by deriving tighter bounds for multivalued PoCs through the incorporation of causal information encoded in covariates and mediators. We illustrate the theoretical results with toy examples, while simulation studies further demonstrate that the proposed bounds are tighter than existing nonbinary bounds.

</details>


### [103] [Designing AI Pipelines for Decision-Ready ITSM Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12670)
*Archan Dutta,Yash Dharmadhikari,Marat Valiullin,Rahul Guha,Alexander Liss*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于设计科学研究原则的社会技术AI流水线，将异构ITSM工单数据转化为多层级决策支持工件，结合LLM模式规范化、HDBSCAN子主题聚类和层次聚类，生成面向高管的主主题和细粒度子主题。利益相关者评估显示四项决策支持指标（可解释性、可操作性、信任度、使用意愿）均平均超过4.0/5.0，信任度为最一致信号，将ITSM分析定位为转换、抽象和以人为中心设计的信息系统问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ITSM系统积累大量异构工单数据，销售和高管利益相关者难以将其转化为可执行的情报，亟需一种能够将原始数据转化为多层级决策支持工件的方法。

Method: 遵循设计科学研究原则，构建社会技术AI流水线：首先利用LLM进行模式规范化处理异构数据，然后应用HDBSCAN进行子主题聚类，再通过层次凝聚聚类生成主主题，最终产出面向高管的主主题和细粒度子主题两级决策支持工件。

Result: 利益相关者评估（六个工件，五位来自销售工程和客户成功角色的评估者）显示，四项决策支持指标——可解释性、可操作性、信任度、使用意愿——平均均超过4.0/5.0，其中信任度表现为最一致的信号。

Conclusion: 研究将ITSM分析定位为信息系统领域的转换、抽象与以人为中心设计问题，验证了结合LLM规范化、密度聚类与层次聚类的社会技术流水线在生成可信、可操作的高管级洞察方面的有效性。

Abstract: IT service management (ITSM) systems accumulate large volumes of heterogeneous ticket data that are difficult for sales and executive stakeholders to convert into actionable intelligence. This paper presents a sociotechnical AI pipeline, designed and evaluated following design science research principles, that transforms raw ITSM exports into a multilevel decision-support artifact. The pipeline combines LLM-based schema normalization, HDBSCAN sub-topic clustering, and hierarchical agglomerative clustering to generate executive-facing Main-topics and granular Sub-topics. A stakeholder evaluation across six artifacts and five raters from Sales Engineering and customer success roles shows that all four decision-support metrics, interpretability, actionability, trust, and likelihood of use, on average exceed 4.0 out of 5.0, with trust as the most consistent signal. The findings position ITSM analytics as an Information Systems (IS) problem of transformation, abstraction, and human-centered design.

</details>


### [104] [On the Expressive Power of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12671)
*Phokion Kolaitis,Rik Sengupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了如何利用电路复杂性理论分析Transformer模型的表达能力，展示了将Transformer参数（注意力、精度等）与电路复杂性类（门类型、规模、深度）对应比较的研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于Transformer已成为所有大语言模型的核心组件，理论计算机科学界需要精确刻画其作为语言识别器的表达能力，将其与经典计算模型进行严格对比。

Method: 采用电路复杂性理论作为分析框架，将Transformer的各种资源参数（如注意力机制、数值精度、层数等）映射到电路复杂性的相应参数（门类型、电路规模、深度等），建立直接对应关系。

Result: 梳理了利用电路复杂性概念和方法界定Transformer表达能力的选定研究结果，揭示了不同资源约束下Transformer的计算能力边界。

Conclusion: 电路复杂性是分析Transformer表达能力的'正确'理论分支，这种参数化对比方法为理解大语言模型的理论基础提供了严格的数学框架。

Abstract: Multi-layer transformers form the critical component of essentially all large language models (LLMs) in use today. Because of their ubiquity and computational capability, there is a rapidly growing body of work that aims to precisely calibrate the expressive power of transformers as language recognizers by comparing them against standard models of computation studied for decades by the theoretical computer science community. In this endeavor, circuit complexity has by and large emerged as the "correct" branch of computational complexity to analyze the expressive power of transformers; the reason is that parameterizing transformers by the various resources they use, such as attention and precision, leads to direct comparisons with different classes of circuits parameterized by resources such as type of gates, size, and depth. Here, we present an overview of selected results that delineate the expressive power of transformers using concepts and methods from circuit complexity.

</details>


### [105] [Lines and Ladders: A Context-Aware Multi-Agent Framework for Large-Scale Retail Price Taxonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12674)
*Ravi Teja Chunduri,Srikaran Reddy Boya,Deep Narayan Mishra,Ajay Kumar B,Karthik Kumaran,Pranay Kona*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可扩展、上下文感知的多智能体框架，利用专业化的LLM智能体自动化构建“Lines and Ladders”定价分类体系，解决全球零售商在海量SKU下维持价格一致性和EDLP策略的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目录包含数百万活跃商品，人工治理价格关系不可行；不同变体间的定价不一致会扭曲客户价值感知并蚕食销售，因此需要自动化、可扩展的解决方案来构建连贯的定价结构。

Method: 采用三智能体系统：分别负责识别关键属性、提取多模态数值、应用分层分组逻辑，协同构建“Lines and Ladders”定价分类体系，缓解单智能体认知过载。

Result: 在真实企业数据上评估并部署生产环境：Lines任务F1达0.83，显著优于单智能体基线；食品与消耗品类别精确率>90%、召回率>75%；非结构化一般商品目录分配准确率80.2%。

Conclusion: 多智能体框架能有效自动化大规模定价分类构建，显著提升价格一致性治理能力，为零售商EDLP策略落地提供可行技术路径。

Abstract: Maintaining price consistency and executing an Every Day Low Price strategy is critical for global retailers. However, with catalogs spanning millions of active items, manual governance of price relationships is infeasible. Inconsistent pricing across item variants distorts customer value perception and cannibalizes sales. To address this, we present a scalable, context-aware Multi-Agent Framework designed to automate the construction of "Lines and Ladders" pricing taxonomies. Our framework employs specialized LLM agents to construct these coherent pricing structures by identifying key attributes, extracting multi-modal values, and applying hierarchical grouping logic. Evaluated on real-world enterprise data and deployed in production, our 3-Agent system achieves an F1-score of 0.83 for Lines, outperforming single-agent baselines by mitigating cognitive overload. The system achieves >90% precision and >75% recall in Food & Consumables, and 80.2% assignment accuracy in the unstructured General Merchandise catalog.

</details>


### [106] [Privacy-Preserving RAG by Concealing Sensitive Information from External LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12675)
*Saleh Almohaimeed,Saad Almohaimeed,Mousa Jari,Fahad Alotaibi,Khalid A. Alobaid*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SEAG框架通过轻量级模型定位敏感实体并生成别名，构建实体替换表，在查询和检索文档发送给外部生成器前替换敏感词，实现隐私保护的RAG，实验显示用户指标准确率超80%，敏感实体隐藏准确率在74%-78%之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG隐私研究主要关注防止未授权用户访问敏感数据，但忽视了外部生成器能直接访问查询和检索文档中可能包含的机密信息，存在信息滥用风险，因此需要一种在利用第三方强力生成器时不泄露敏感信息的框架。

Method: 提出敏感实体别名生成器（SEAG）框架，包含轻量级模型：定位敏感实体、生成对应别名、构建实体替换表；在将用户查询和检索文档发送给外部生成器前，使用替换表替换敏感词。构建两个数据集：一个用于微调SEAG模型生成替换表，另一个用于评估整个框架。

Result: 实验表明SEAG框架成功。用户指标（衡量模型在向用户提供正确响应同时向外部生成器隐藏敏感信息的能力）上，所有SEAG模型准确率均超80%。额外分析评估Qwen-3、LLaMA-3.2、Phi-4三个SEAG模型在给定文档中隐藏所有敏感实体的能力，总准确率分别为77.83%、76.73%、74.91%。

Conclusion: SEAG框架有效解决了RAG场景下外部生成器访问敏感信息的隐私问题，通过轻量级模型实现敏感实体别名替换，在保持回答正确性的前提下实现了较高的隐私保护效果，为隐私保护的RAG提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to improve the performance of Large Language Models (LLMs) in answering user queries. Existing privacy research on RAG has focused on preventing unauthorized users from accessing sensitive data. However, another important problem that is often overlooked in RAG privacy research is that external generators have access to the query and the retrieved documents, which may contain confidential information that could potentially be misused or accessed for unintended purposes. In this paper, we introduce the Sensitive Entity Alias Generator (SEAG), a privacy-preserving framework that empowers users to utilize powerful third-party generators without disclosing sensitive information. SEAG introduces a lightweight model that locates sensitive entities, generates corresponding aliases, and constructs an entity replacement table. The table is used to replace sensitive words in the user's query and in the retrieved documents before they are forwarded to an external generator. For this purpose, two datasets were constructed: one for fine-tuning SEAG models to generate entity replacement tables, and another for evaluating the entire SEAG framework. The experimental results demonstrate the success of the SEAG framework. As for the User metric, which measures the ability of the model to provide a correct response to the user while hiding sensitive information from the external generator, all SEAG models achieved over 80% accuracy. Additional analysis further evaluated the ability of SEAG models Qwen-3, LLaMA-3.2, and Phi-4 to hide all sensitive entities within given documents. The results show good performance with total accuracies of 77.83%, 76.73%, and 74.91%, respectively.

</details>


### [107] [The Role of Natural Language Understanding in Multimodal Video-Based Dengue Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12677)
*Danial Sharifrazi,Saadat Behzadi,Julakha Jahan Jui,Mojtaba Mohammadi,Nouman Javed,Roohallah Alizadehsani,Prasad N. Paradkar,Asim Bhatti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于YOLO和CLIP的视觉-语言框架，用于从视频数据中分类未感染与登革病毒2型（DENV2）感染蚊子的飞行帧，实现了98.54%的帧级准确率和99.91%的灵敏度，时间聚合后达到完美的视频级性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蚊子体型小、运动快且不规则，加上背景、光照、阴影等环境因素干扰，导致从视频中可靠提取感染相关行为特征极具挑战性，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: 首先使用YOLO从背景中分离蚊子区域；然后将视频帧的视觉特征与生物学意义的文本提示在共享嵌入空间中对齐；采用监督双向对比学习微调多模态模型，并基于帧级图像-文本相似度进行分类。

Result: 帧级分类准确率98.54%，灵敏度99.91%；时间聚合后视频级性能达到100%；消融实验表明微调和基于CLIP的表征至关重要，文本分支提供语义对齐而非准确率提升。

Conclusion: 视觉-语言模型为从视频数据分析感染相关的生物学行为提供了一个有效的框架，展示了多模态学习在精细行为分类任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Detecting infection-related behavioral changes in mosquitoes from video data is challenging because mosquitoes are small, move rapidly and irregularly, and are affected by environmental factors such as background, lighting, and shadows, which can make reliable feature extraction difficult. In this study, a YOLO- and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP)-based vision-language framework is proposed to classify mosquito flight frames of uninfected and Dengue virus serotype 2 (DENV2)-infected mosquitoes. First, YOLO is used to isolate mosquito regions from the background. Then, visual features extracted from video frames are aligned with biologically meaningful textual prompts in a shared embedding space. The multimodal model was fine-tuned using supervised bidirectional contrastive learning and evaluated through frame-level image-text similarity-based classification. The results show that the proposed method achieved 98.54% accuracy and 99.91% sensitivity at the frame level. After temporal aggregation of frame-level information, the model achieved complete video-level performance. The ablation results showed that fine-tuning and CLIP-based representations were essential for this domain, while the textual branch provided semantic image-text alignment rather than an accuracy advantage over the vision-only model. These findings suggest that vision-language models can provide a useful framework for analyzing infection-related biological behaviors from video data.

</details>
